The user wants me to extract a compliance matrix from the provided RFP text.
I need to follow the specific rules provided in the system prompt:
1.  **verbatim_text**: Exact sentence(s) from the RFP. No paraphrasing.
2.  **One requirement per row**: Split sentences with multiple obligations.
3.  **section_ref**: RFP's own numbering (e.g., "3.2.1").
4.  **type**: "shall" (binding), "should" (advisory), "info" (administrative facts).
5.  **category**: eligibility, deliverable, deadline, form, evaluation. Deadlines with dates get "deadline" and ISO due_date.
6.  **page**: PDF page number (1-indexed).
7.  **id**: Sequential R-001, R-002, etc.
8.  **Do NOT invent requirements**: Only extract what is explicitly stated.

I need to scan the provided text (pages 1-18 of the RFP) for requirements.
The text provided ends at page 18, section 3.4.1. I need to make sure I cover everything provided in the input.

- "Prior to award of the Contract, Respondent must obtain TX-RAMP Level 2 certification." -> Eligibility.
- "TX-RAMP Provisional Status does not satisfy the TX-RAMP Certification Level 2 requirement." -> Info.
- "For this Solicitation, HHSC shall not provide 'agency-sponsored' TX-RAMP Provisional Status certification to any Respondent." -> Info.

**Page 11:**
- "Contractor must provide Services that comply with TX-RAMP Certification Level 2." -> Deliverable/Eligibility.
- "Contractor shall comply with Exhibit N, Requirements Traceability Matrix..." -> Deliverable.
- "On or before the ninetieth (90th) Calendar Day following each Disaster Recovery test, Contractor will provide DSHS, with a written summary report detailing the results of the Disaster Recovery test." -> Deliverable/Deadline.
- "Upon DSHS's written request, Contractor shall make Contractor personnel available to discuss with DSHS the results of the Disaster Recovery test." -> Deliverable.
- "Contractor shall use commercially reasonable efforts to resolve issues identified during the Disaster Recovery test..." -> Deliverable.
- "Contractor shall conduct on an annual basis a Disaster Recovery test..." -> Deliverable/Deadline.
- "The Contractor shall submit an invoice to DSHS in a format consistent with the line-item descriptions of Exhibit M, Cost Proposal." -> Deliverable.
- "Each invoice submitted must identify: a. The purchase order number; b. HHSC's Contract number; c. The time period (e.g., month); and d. Line-item description for Service or Deliverable." -> Deliverable/Form.
- "Invoices shall be sent to: Health and Human Services Commission Attn: Invoice – HHSC MC2065 4601 W. Guadalupe St. Austin, TX 78751 Fax: (512) 206-4854 E-mail: IT_invoicing@hhs.texas.gov" -> Deliverable/Form.

- "Respondents must complete and return with their Solicitation Response Exhibit D-1, Attachment 2 to the DUA, Security and Privacy Inquiry (SPI)." -> Form.

Question for HHSC." -> Form.

    - "The standards of conduct must include the ten standards of ethical conduct set forth in Section I of the HHS Ethics Policy and requirements to comply with ethical standards set forth in federal and state law (including, but not limited to, Title 1 of the Texas Administrative Code Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter D, Rule §391.405(a))." -> Deliverable.
    - "Standards of conduct of any Contractor, Respondent, or vendor may be reviewed and/or audited by the State Auditor and HHSC, The Contractor, Respondent, or vendor must cooperate with the review and/or audit." -> Binding.
    - "Additionally, pursuant to Title 1 of the Texas Administrative Code Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter D, Rule §391.405(a), HHSC may examine a Respondent’s standards of conduct in the evaluation of a bid, offer, proposal, quote, or other applicable expression of interest in a proposed purchase of Goods or Services." -> Info.
    - "Any vendor, Contractor, or Subcontractor, that violates a provision of Title 1 of the Texas Administrative Code Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter D may be barred from receiving future contracts or have an existing contract canceled." -> Info.
    - "Additionally, HHSC may report the vendor’s actions to the Comptroller of Public Accounts for statewide debarment, or law enforcement." -> Info.
- 8.1.8 Disclosure of Interested Parties (continued from page 37)
    - "Pursuant to Section 2252.908 of the Texas Government Code, a successful Respondent to be awarded a Contract with a value of $1 million or more or awarded a Contract that would require the successful Respondent to register as a lobbyist under Texas Government Code Chapter 305 must submit a disclosure of interested parties form to HHSC at the time the Respondent submits the signed Contract." -> Deliverable.
    - "Rules and filing instructions may be found on the Texas Ethics Commission's public website and additional instructions will be given by HHSC to the successful Respondent." -> Info.
- 8.2 INSURANCE (continued from page 37)
    - 8.2.1 Required Coverage
        - "For the duration of any Contract resulting from this Solicitation, the successful Respondent shall acquire insurance, with financially sound and reputable independent insurers, in the type and amount listed in Exhibit R, Insurance Requirements." -> Binding.
        - "If coverage expires during the term of the Contract, renewal certificates for each type of coverage must be provided to System Agency." -> Binding.
        - "Failure to maintain insurance coverage or acceptable alternative methods of insurance shall be deemed a breach of Contract." -> Binding.
        - "In the event that any policy is determined by the System Agency to be deficient to comply with the terms of this Contract, System Agency may require additional policies or coverage as the System Agency may reasonably request or that are required by law or regulation." -> Info.
    - 8.2.2 Alternative Insurability (continued from page 37)
        - "Notwithstanding the preceding, HHSC reserves the right to consider reasonable alternative methods of insuring the Contract in lieu of the insurance policies customarily required." -> Info.
        - "It will be the Respondent's responsibility to recommend to HHSC alternative methods of insuring the Contract." -> Binding.
        - "Any alternatives proposed by Respondent should be accompanied by a detailed explanation regarding Respondent's inability to obtain the required insurance and/or bonds." -> Advisory ("should").
        - "HHSC shall be the sole and final judge as to the adequacy of any substitute form of insurance coverage." -> Info.
- 8.3 PROTEST (continued from page 37)
    - "Any protest shall be governed by the rules published by HHSC in the Texas Administrative Code Title 1, Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter C (Protests)." -> Info.

    - "Respondents understand that issuance of this Solicitation in no way constitutes a commitment by HHSC to award a Contract or to pay any costs incurred by a Respondent in the preparation of a response to this Solicitation." -> Info.
    - "HHSC is not liable for any costs incurred by a Respondent." -> Info.
    - "Costs of developing Solicitation Responses, preparing for or participating in Oral Presentations, Demonstrations, and Site Visits, or any other similar expenses incurred by a Respondent are entirely the responsibility of the Respondent, and will not be reimbursed in any manner by the State of Texas." -> Info.
- 8.1.4 Contract Responsibility (continued from page 41)
    - "HHSC will look solely to the successful Respondent for the performance of all contractual obligations that may result from an award based on this Solicitation." -> Info.
    - "The successful Respondent shall not be relieved of its obligations for any nonperformance by its Subcontractors." -> Info.
- 8.1.5 Public Information Act - Respondent Requirements Regarding Disclosure (continued from page 41)
    - "Proposals and contracts are subject to the Texas Public Information Act (PIA)." -> Info.
    - "Texas Government Code Chapter 552, and may be disclosed to the public upon request." -> Info.
    - "Other legal authority also requires HHSC to post contracts and proposals on its public website and to provide such information to the Legislative Budget Board for posting on its public website." -> Info.
    - "Under the PIA, certain information is protected from public release." -> Info.
    - "If Respondent asserts that information provided in its Solicitation Response is exempt from disclosure under the PIA, Respondent must:" -> Binding.
    - "a. Mark Original Solicitation Response:" -> Deliverable.
    - "1. Mark the Original Solicitation Response, on the top of the front page, with the words 'CONTAINS CONFIDENTIAL INFORMATION' in large, bold, capitalized letters (the size of, or equivalent to, 12-point Times New Roman font or larger);" -> Deliverable.
    - "1. Identify, adjacent to each portion of the Solicitation Response that Respondent claims is exempt from public disclosure, the claimed exemption from disclosure (NOTE: no redactions are to be made in the Original Solicitation Response);" -> Deliverable.
    - "b. Certify in Original Solicitation Response – HHS Solicitation Affirmations (attached as Exhibit A to this Solicitation):" -> Deliverable.
    - "Certify, in the designated section of the HHS Solicitation Affirmations, Respondent’s confidential information assertion and the filing of its Public Information Act Copy;" -> Deliverable.
    - "c. Submit Public Information Act Copy of Solicitation Response:" -> Deliverable.
    - "Submit a separate 'Public Information Act Copy' of the Original Solicitation Response (in addition to the original and all copies otherwise required under the provisions of this Solicitation)." -> Deliverable.
    - "1. The copy must be clearly marked as 'Public Information Act Copy' on the front page in large, bold, capitalized letters (the size of, or equivalent to, 12-point Times New Roman font or larger);" -> Deliverable.
    - "2. Each portion Respondent claims is exempt from public disclosure must be redacted (blacked out);" -> Deliverable.
    - "3. Respondent must identify, adjacent to each redaction, the claimed exemption from disclosure. Each identification provided as required in Subsection (c) of this section must be identical to those set forth in the Original Solicitation Response as required in Subsection (a)(2), above." -> Deliverable.
    - "The only difference in required markings and information between the Original Solicitation Response and the 'Public Information Act Copy' of the Solicitation Response will be redactions - which can only be included in the 'Public Information Act Copy.' There must be no redactions in the Original Solicitation Response." -> Deliverable.
    - "By submitting a response to this Solicitation, Respondent agrees that, if Respondent does not mark the Original Solicitation Response, provide the required certification in the Affirmations and Solicitation Acceptance, and submit the Public Information Act Copy, Respondent’s Solicitation Response will be considered to be public information that may be released to the public without notice to the Respondent in any manner including, but not limited to, in accordance with the Public Information Act, posted on HHSC’s public website, and posted on the Legislative Budget Board’s public website." -> Info.
    - "If any or all Respondents submit partial, but not complete, information suggesting inclusion of confidential information and failure to comply with the requirements set forth in this section, HHSC, in its sole discretion and in any Solicitation, reserves the right to (1) disqualify all Respondents that fail to fully comply with the requirements set forth in this section, or (2) to offer all Respondents that fail to comply with the requirements set forth in this section additional time to comply." -> Info/Binding.
    - "Respondent should not submit a Public Information Act Copy indicating that the entire Solicitation Response is exempt from disclosure." -> Info.
    - "Merely making a blanket claim that the entire response is protected from disclosure because it contains any amount of confidential, proprietary, trade secret, or privileged information is not acceptable, and may make the entire proposal subject to release under the PIA." -> Info.
    - "A Solicitation Response should not be marked or asserted as copyrighted material." -> Info.
    - "If Respondent asserts a copyright to any portion of its response, by submitting a response, Respondent agrees to reproduction and posting on public websites by the State of Texas, including HHSC and all other state agencies, without cost or liability." -> Info.
    - "HHSC will strictly adhere to the requirements of the PIA regarding the disclosure of public information." -> Info.
    - "As a result, by participating in this Solicitation process, Respondent acknowledges that all information, documentation, and other materials submitted in the Solicitation Response in response to this Solicitation may be subject to public disclosure under the PIA." -> Info.
    - "HHSC does not have authority to agree that any information submitted will not be subject to disclosure." -> Info.
    - "Disclosure is governed by the PIA and by rulings of the Office of the Texas Attorney General." -> Info.
    - "Respondents are advised to consult with their legal counsel concerning disclosure issues resulting from this process and to take precautions to safeguard trade secrets and proprietary or otherwise confidential information." -> Info.
    - "HHSC no obligation or responsibility relating to the disclosure or nondisclosure of information submitted by Respondents." -> Info.
    - "For more information concerning the types of information that may be withheld under the PIA or questions about the PIA, please refer to the most current Public Information Act Handbook published by the Office of the Texas Attorney General or contact the attorney general’s Open Government Hotline at (512) 478-OPEN (6736) or toll-free at (877) 673-6839 (877-OPEN TEX). To access and download the current Public Information Act Handbook, please visit the attorney general’s website at https://www.texasattorneygeneral.gov/open-government/members-pub" -> Info.

    - "Submit a separate 'Public Information Act Copy' of the Original Solicitation Response (in addition to the original and all copies otherwise required under the provisions of this Solicitation)." -> Deliverable.
    - "1. The copy must be clearly marked as 'Public Information Act Copy' on the front page in large, bold, capitalized letters (the size of, or equivalent to, 12-point Times New Roman font or larger);" -> Deliverable.
    - "2. Each portion Respondent claims is exempt from public disclosure must be redacted (blacked out);" -> Deliverable.
    - "3. Respondent must identify, adjacent to each redaction, the claimed exemption from disclosure. Each identification provided as required in Subsection (c) of this section must be identical to those set forth in the Original Solicitation Response as required in Subsection (a)(2), above." -> Deliverable.
    - "The only difference in required markings and information between the Original Solicitation Response and the 'Public Information Act Copy' of the Solicitation Response will be redactions - which can only be included in the 'Public Information Act Copy.' There must be no redactions in the Original Solicitation Response." -> Deliverable.
    - "By submitting a response to this Solicitation, Respondent agrees that, if Respondent does not mark the Original Solicitation Response, provide the required certification in the Affirmations and Solicitation Acceptance, and submit the Public Information Act Copy, Respondent’s Solicitation Response will be considered to be public information that may be released to the public without notice to the Respondent in any manner including, but not limited to, in accordance with the Public Information Act, posted on HHSC’s public website, and posted on the Legislative Budget Board’s public website." -> Info.
    - "If any or all Respondents submit partial, but not complete, information suggesting inclusion of confidential information and failure to comply with the requirements set forth in this section, HHSC, in its sole discretion and in any Solicitation, reserves the right to (1) disqualify all Respondents that fail to fully comply with the requirements set forth in this section, or (2) to offer all Respondents that fail to comply with the requirements set forth in this section additional time to comply." -> Info/Binding.
    - "Respondent should not submit a Public Information Act Copy indicating that the entire Solicitation Response is exempt from disclosure." -> Info.
    - "Merely making a blanket claim that the entire response is protected from disclosure because it contains any amount of confidential, proprietary, trade secret, or privileged information is not acceptable, and may make the entire proposal subject to release under the PIA." -> Info.
    - "A Solicitation Response should not be marked or asserted as copyrighted material." -> Info.
    - "If Respondent asserts a copyright to any portion of its response, by submitting a response, Respondent agrees to reproduction and posting on public websites by the State of Texas, including HHSC and all other state agencies, without cost or liability." -> Info.
    - "HHSC will strictly adhere to the requirements of the PIA regarding the disclosure of public information." -> Info.
    - "As a result, by participating in this Solicitation process, Respondent acknowledges that all information, documentation, and other materials submitted in the Solicitation Response in response to this Solicitation may be subject to public disclosure under the PIA." -> Info.
    - "HHSC does not have authority to agree that any information submitted will not be subject to disclosure." -> Info.
    - "Disclosure is governed by the PIA and by rulings of the Office of the Texas Attorney General." -> Info.
    - "Respondents are advised to consult with their legal counsel concerning disclosure issues resulting from this process and to take precautions to safeguard trade secrets and proprietary or otherwise confidential information." -> Info.
    - "HHSC no obligation or responsibility relating to the disclosure or nondisclosure of information submitted by Respondents." -> Info.
    - "For more information concerning the types of information that may be withheld under the PIA or questions about the PIA, please refer to the most current Public Information Act Handbook published by the Office of the Texas Attorney General or contact the attorney general’s Open Government Hotline at (512) 478-OPEN (6736) or toll-free at (877) 673-6839 (877-OPEN TEX). To access and download the current Public Information Act Handbook, please visit the attorney general’s website at https://www.texasattorneygeneral.gov/open-government/members-pub" -> Info.

    - "SUBMISSION OF ANY DOCUMENT TO HHSC IN RESPONSE TO THIS SOLICITATION CONSTITUTES AN IRREVOCABLE WAIVER AND AGREEMENT BY RESPONDENT TO FULLY INDEMNIFY THE STATE OF TEXAS, HHSC FROM ANY CLAIM OF INFRINGEMENT BY HHSC REGARDING THE INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY RIGHTS OF RESPONDENT OR ANY THIRD PARTY FOR ANY MATERIALS SUBMITTED TO HHSC BY RESPONDENT." -> Binding.
- 8.1.7 Standards of Conduct for Vendors (continued from page 43)
    - "Pursuant to Title 1 of the Texas Administrative Code Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter D, Rule §391.405(a), Contractors, Respondents, and vendors interested in working with HHSC are required to implement standards of conduct for their own personnel and agents on terms at least as restrictive as those applicable to HHSC." -> Binding.
    - "These standards must adhere to ethics requirements adopted in rule, in addition to any ethics policy, or code of ethics approved by the HHSC Executive Commissioner." -> Binding.
    - "A Respondent must sign and submit all ethics, disclosure, confidentiality, and other forms required under the procurement and any resulting contract." -> Binding.
    - "The standards of conduct must include the ten standards of ethical conduct set forth in Section I of the HHS Ethics Policy and requirements to comply with ethical standards set forth in federal and state law (including, but not limited to, Title 1 of the Texas Administrative Code Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter D, Rule §391.405(a))." -> Deliverable.
    - "Standards of conduct of any Contractor, Respondent, or vendor may be reviewed and/or audited by the State Auditor and HHSC, The Contractor, Respondent, or vendor must cooperate with the review and/or audit." -> Binding.
    - "Additionally, pursuant to Title 1 of the Texas Administrative Code Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter D, Rule §391.405(a), HHSC may examine a Respondent’s standards of conduct in the evaluation of a bid, offer, proposal, quote, or other applicable expression of interest in a proposed purchase of Goods or Services." -> Info.
    - "Any vendor, Contractor, or Subcontractor, that violates a provision of Title 1 of the Texas Administrative Code Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter D may be barred from receiving future contracts or have an existing contract canceled." -> Info.
    - "Additionally, HHSC may report the vendor’s actions to the Comptroller of Public Accounts for statewide debarment, or law enforcement." -> Info.
- 8.1.8 Disclosure of Interested Parties (continued from page 43)
    - "Pursuant to Section 2252.908 of the Texas Government Code, a successful Respondent to be awarded a Contract with a value of $1 million or more or awarded a Contract that would require the successful Respondent to register as a lobbyist under Texas Government Code Chapter 305 must submit a disclosure of interested parties form to HHSC at the time the Respondent submits the signed Contract." -> Deliverable.
    - "Rules and filing instructions may be found on the Texas Ethics Commission's public website and additional instructions will be given by HHSC to the successful Respondent." -> Info.
- 8.2 INSURANCE (continued from page 43)
    - 8.2.1 Required Coverage
        - "For the duration of any Contract resulting from this Solicitation, the successful Respondent shall acquire insurance, with financially sound and reputable independent insurers, in the type and amount listed in Exhibit R, Insurance Requirements." -> Binding.
        - "If coverage expires during the term of the Contract, renewal certificates for each type of coverage must be provided to System Agency." -> Binding.
        - "Failure to maintain insurance coverage or acceptable alternative methods of insurance shall be deemed a breach of Contract." -> Binding.
        - "In the event that any policy is determined by the System Agency to be deficient to comply with the terms of this Contract, System Agency may require additional policies or coverage as the System Agency may reasonably request or that are required by law or regulation." -> Info.
    - 8.2.2 Alternative Insurability (continued from page 43)
        - "Notwithstanding the preceding, HHSC reserves the right to consider reasonable alternative methods of insuring the Contract in lieu of the insurance policies customarily required." -> Info.
        - "It will be the Respondent's responsibility to recommend to HHSC alternative methods of insuring the Contract." -> Binding.
        - "Any alternatives proposed by Respondent should be accompanied by a detailed explanation regarding Respondent's inability to obtain the required insurance and/or bonds." -> Advisory ("should").
        - "HHSC shall be the sole and final judge as to the adequacy of any substitute form of insurance coverage." -> Info.
- 8.3 PROTEST (continued from page 43)
    - "Any protest shall be governed by the rules published by HHSC in the Texas Administrative Code Title 1, Part 15, Chapter 391, Subchapter C (Protests)." -> Info.
